kernel/scripts/lib_ck_m1_checks.sh	Stricter than qemu_boot_test.sh (which fails only on 124): PSCI SYSTEM_RESET under -no-reboot makes QEMU exit 0, so any other status is a QEMU crash or abort even if the markers were already flushed.
kernel/scripts/lib_gate_hold.sh	- a dead holder's record is removed by the same gate (same <name>) at once, or by any gate once its expected_end has passed (quietlock's stale rule);
kernel/scripts/lib_gate_hold.sh	- a gate removes only its own record at exit (exact line match).
kernel/scripts/lib_gate_hold.sh	The machine quiet flag (AIENOS_QUIET_FLAG, default ~/workspace/.spark-quiet) belongs to quietlock: gates only READ it (no agent raises it without Drake's approval; holds are taken with `quietlock hold`).
kernel/scripts/lib_verify_summary.sh	A skipped step makes the verdict NOT_RUN for that step (never "PASS"), and AIENOS_STRICT=1 turns any skipped step into a failure.
kernel/scripts/lib_verify_summary.sh	QEMU steps are emulator evidence only; hardware is never checked by verify_all.sh.
kernel/scripts/lib_verify_summary.sh	run_or_skip DESCRIPTION COMMAND...: a script that exits 3 reports NOT_RUN (it skipped a check class); that is a skipped step here, never a pass.
kernel/scripts/m3_receipt.sh	Exit status: 0 all proofs present (or --allow-missing), 1 verification failed or the run changed tracked files, 2 usage or dirty tree, 3 proofs MISSING without --allow-missing.
kernel/scripts/m3_receipt.sh	It refuses to run on a dirty tree, records the commit and the toolchain, runs `AIENOS_STRICT=1 bash scripts/verify_all.sh` (so no step may be skipped) with every serial log captured, and writes evidence/m3_receipt_<YYYY-MM-DD>.md with the PASS lines grouped by proof, the sha256 and line count of every raw log, the limitations, and an operator sign-off line that stays "pending" until the operator writes "M3 accepted".
kernel/scripts/native_boot_preflight.sh	Checks, one line each (PASS / STOP / INFO); the last line is NATIVE_PREFLIGHT: READY every STOP-class check passed NATIVE_PREFLIGHT: NOT_READY at least one STOP line (exit 1) Items Drake does in person (at the machine, display and keyboard attached, fresh go-ahead for this boot) cannot be read by a script: they are printed as ASK lines and never counted as passed.
kernel/scripts/native_package.sh	--allow-dirty (tests only): build from a dirty tree, recorded as tree_clean no; verify and sign refuse such a package unless given --allow-dirty too (sign-test does not refuse it: the TEST key is never for hardware).
kernel/scripts/native_package.sh	Any CK_* variable in the environment is refused: every make variable that selects the image is passed explicitly and recorded.
kernel/scripts/native_package.sh	A real signature never goes on a dirty-tree package (--allow-dirty: tests only); TEST signing may.
kernel/scripts/native_package.sh	A tracked-only dirty check cannot attest those extra build inputs.
kernel/scripts/native_package.sh	Every refusal prints "refused: <reason>" and exits 1.
kernel/scripts/native_package.sh	hardware-staging: the owner public keys file is the loader's trusted-key table (ck_slot_tool keys-c refuses TEST keys in it); core and full: the labelled RFC 8032 TEST key loader (never for hardware).
kernel/scripts/native_package.sh	loader/SLOTLDR.EFI the signed A/B slot loader (P11 cut 3), built twice like the image: owner keys (CK_SLOT_KEYS = --owner-pubkeys) for hardware-staging, the labelled TEST key otherwise SHA256SUMS sha256sum lines over image/BOOTAA64.EFI, loader/SLOTLDR.EFI and PACKAGE.txt PROVENANCE.txt "package_sha256 <sha256(SHA256SUMS)>": the resulting package digest, which is also the slot manifest's provenance field (it cannot live in PACKAGE.txt, which SHA256SUMS covers) slot/MANIFEST.bin unsigned slot manifest (native/boot/slot_format.h); its provenance field is package_sha256, so the owner's one signature over the manifest binds the whole package slot/MANIFEST.tbs the 448 bytes the owner signs offline, written by `tbs --pubkey` (or `build --pubkey`): signer_id = SHA-256(pubkey) is inside the signed bytes.
kernel/scripts/native_package.sh	openssl pkeyutl -sign -inkey owner_root.key.pem -rawin \ -in MANIFEST.tbs -out MANIFEST.sig slot/MANIFEST.signed.bin after `sign` (or `sign-test`, TEST key only)

kernel/scripts/qemu_ck_rollback_test.sh	Each case is a lane with its own fresh copy of AAVMF_VARS.fd (the emulated UEFI variable store) and its own ESP folder, four boots: boot 1 stage the mock stager sets BootNext=0000 and resets boot 2 attempt firmware consumes BootNext and starts (or fails to load) Boot0000 boot 3 default the next boot (for rejected/absent the Default already ran in boot 2, so boot 3 is its first repeat) boot 4 default one more boot: still Default, no second candidate attempt After every boot the host reads the variable store file itself (native/kernel/tools/ck_uefi_vars.c, read-only), so the BootNext and BootOrder verdicts come from the firmware's own store, not from a guest print.

kernel/tools/imac-image/build-efi.sh	Every source must have its d4ffde3c sha256 below, else this exits 3 (NOT_RUN) and builds nothing: other sources would publish a different EFI.
kernel/tools/imac-image/README.md	A refusal publishes nothing (compile goes to a scratch file and is linked into place only on success), so a retry at the same path succeeds and `imac-image` can never pick up a zero-byte EFI.
kernel/tools/imac-image/README.md	The payload is streamed one 512-byte cluster at a time, copied from the input and written straight into the image, so there is no in-memory size ceiling: a 1.5 MiB EFI and a 3 MiB EFI both build, and the only limit is the fixed ESP geometry, which is checked before anything is written.
kernel/tools/imac-image/README.md	The script carries the sha256 of each at `d4ffde3c` and refuses (NOT_RUN, exit 3) any source that is missing or differs; `native/omega/allen` here differs in six of them.

tests/gates/run.sh	Every step that builds against physics takes the commit from physics.lock, never a literal: the Makefile's check-physics-lock refuses any other commit, so a literal here goes red on the next lock bump (it did for the e95e3ed bump, omega#508, and again for 048e5f5, omega#523).

tools/effect_cap64_receipt.sh	The receipt says FAIL (and lists why) when any suite fails, the tree is dirty or changes during the run, or the pins do not match.
tools/evidence_immutable.sh	Before the check it runs its controls (CONTROLS below) on throwaway repositories, each a way through that a reviewer or the author tried, and fails unless every one gives its expected exit.
tools/evidence_immutable.sh	Every control, by name; each must run and give its expected exit (asserted, not only printed).
tools/evidence_immutable.sh	kernel/ is not yet on the base, so this is what keeps a kernel/ file added after the import: it cannot be deleted or rewritten, an edit reverted later still fails, and a merge cannot bring in other bytes through an unrelated root commit (the way the import itself came in).
tools/evidence_immutable.sh	NOT_RUN is never a pass.
tools/evidence_immutable.sh	The environment cannot point the check elsewhere: every GIT_* variable but GIT_CEILING_DIRECTORIES is cleared, and replace refs and grafts are ignored.
